479→    handlers_.push_back({prefix, help_text, callback, removable, mutates_state, params});\n   480→    return true;\n   481→  }\n   482→  return false;\n   483→}\n   484→\n   485→bool Request Routing and Handler Matching**\n**Location:** `/workspace/envoy/source/server/admin/admin.cc` (Lines 381-412)\n\n```cpp\nAdmin::RequestPtr AdminImpl::makeRequest(AdminStream& admin_stream) const {\n  absl::string_view path_and_query = admin_stream.getRequestHeaders().getPathValue();\n  std::string::size_type query_index = path_and_query.find('?');\n  if (query_index == std::string::npos) {\n    query_index = path_and_query.size();\n  }\n\n  for (const UrlHandler& handler : handlers_) {\n    if (path_and_query.compare(0, query_index, handler.prefix_) == 0) {\n      if (handler.mutates_server_state_) {\n        const absl::string_view method = admin_stream.getRequestHeaders().getMethodValue();\n        if (method != Http::Headers::get().MethodValues.Post) {\n          ENVOY_LOG(error, \"admin path \\\"{}\\\" mutates state, method={} rather than POST\",\n                    handler.prefix_, method);\n          return Admin::makeStaticTextRequest(\n              fmt::format(\"Method {} not allowed, POST required.\", method),\n              Http::Code::MethodNotAllowed);\n        }\n      }\n\n      ASSERT(admin_stream.getRequestHeaders().getPathValue() == path_and_query);\n      return handler.handler_(admin_stream);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Extra space is emitted below to have \"invalid path.\" be a separate sentence in the\n  // 404 output from \"admin commands are:\" in handlerHelp.\n  Buffer::OwnedImpl error_response;\n  error_response.add(\"invalid path. \");\n  getHelp(error_response);\n  return Admin::makeStaticTextRequest(error_response, Http::Code::NotFound);\n}\n```\n\n**Routing Logic:**\n1. Extracts path (before '?') from request\n2. Performs linear prefix matching on handlers list\n3. Validates HTTP method for state-mutating handlers (must be POST)\n4. Returns handler's Request object or 404 if no match\n\n---\n\n### **9. Handler Registration Methods**\n**Location:** `/workspace/envoy/source/server/admin/admin.cc` (Lines 452-500)\n\n```cpp\nbool AdminImpl::addStreamingHandler(const std::string& prefix, const std::string& help_text,\n                                   GenRequestFn callback, bool removable, bool mutates_state,\n                                   const ParamDescriptorVec& params) {\n  ASSERT(prefix.size() > 1);\n  ASSERT(prefix[0] == '/');\n\n  // Sanitize prefix and help_text to ensure no XSS can be injected\n  const std::string::size_type pos = prefix.find_first_of(\"&\\\"'<>?:\");\n  if (pos != std::string::npos) {\n    ENVOY_LOG(error, \"filter \\\"{}\\\" contains invalid character '{}'\", prefix, prefix[pos]);\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  auto it = std::find_if(handlers_.cbegin(), handlers_.cend(),\n                         [&prefix](const UrlHandler& entry) { return prefix == entry.prefix_; });\n  if (it == handlers_.end()) {\n    handlers_.push_back({prefix, help_text, callback, removable, mutates_state, params});\n    return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\nbool AdminImpl::addHandler(const std::string& prefix, const std::string& help_text,\n                          HandlerCb callback, bool removable, bool mutates_state,\n                          const ParamDescriptorVec& params) {\n  return addStreamingHandler(prefix, help_text, RequestGasket::makeGen(callback), removable,\n                             mutates_state, params);\n}\n```\n\n**Validation:**\n- Prefix must start with '/' and have at least one more character\n- XSS protection: rejects characters: `&\"'<>?:`\n- Duplicate prefixes are rejected\n- Sanitized before insertion\n\n---\n\n### **10. Admin Filter Implementation**\n**Location:** `/workspace/envoy/source/server/admin/admin_filter.cc` (Lines 83-107)\n\n```cpp\nvoid AdminFilter::onComplete() {\n  absl::string_view path = request_headers_->getPathValue();\n  ENVOY_STREAM_LOG(debug, \"request complete: path: {}\", *decoder_callbacks_, path);\n\n  auto header_map = Http::ResponseHeaderMapImpl::create();\n  RELEASE_ASSERT(request_headers_, \"\");\n  Admin::RequestPtr handler = admin_.makeRequest(*this);\n  Http::Code code = handler->start(*header_map);\n  Utility::populateFallbackResponseHeaders(code, *header_map);\n  decoder_callbacks_->encodeHeaders(std::move(header_map), false,\n                                    StreamInfo::ResponseCodeDetails::get().AdminFilterResponse);\n\n  // Streaming response loop\n  bool more_data;\n  do {\n    Buffer::OwnedImpl response;\n    more_data = handler->nextChunk(response);\n    bool end_stream = end_stream_on_complete_ && !more_data;\n    ENVOY_LOG_MISC(debug, \"nextChunk: response.length={} more_data={} end_stream={}\",\n                   response.length(), more_data, end_stream);\n    if (response.length() > 0 || end_stream) {\n      decoder_callbacks_->encodeData(response, end_stream);\n    }\n  } while (more_data);\n}\n```\n\n**Request Flow:**\n1. When request is complete, calls `admin_.makeRequest(*this)` to get handler\n2. Calls `handler->start()` to get initial response code\n3. Streams response chunks via `handler->nextChunk()` loop\n4. Sends response headers and data to client\n\n---\n\n### **11. When request is complete, calls `admin_.makeRequest(*this)` to get handler\n2. Calls `handler->start()` to get initial response code\n3. Streams response chunks via `handler->nextChunk()` loop\n4. `/workspace/envoy/source/common/router/router.cc` (lines 431+)\n\nData plane HTTP requests follow this dispatch path:\n\n```\nConnection receives data\n  ↓\nHTTP Connection Manager (NetworkFilter) decodes HTTP\n  ↓\nHTTP filters chain processes request\n  ↓\nRouter Filter::decodeHeaders() called\n  ↓\ncallbacks_->route() - queries route configuration\n  ↓\nIf route found: route_->routeEntry() extracted\n  ↓\nroute_entry_->clusterName() determines upstream cluster\n  ↓\nUpstream connection established and request forwarded\n```\n\nThe Router filter (in `decodeHeaders`) at line 454 calls `callbacks_->route()` which performs route table lookup based on:\n- Request path\n- Request method\n- Host headers\n- Other route matching criteria\n\nIf no route is found, a 404 response is sent locally (line 460).\n\n### 2. **Admin vs Data Plane Listener Creation**\n\n**Admin Listener:** `/workspace/envoy/source/server/admin/admin.h` and `/workspace/envoy/source/server/admin/admin.cc`\n\n**Data Plane Listener:** `/workspace/envoy/source/common/listener_manager/listener_manager_impl.h`\n\n#### Key Separation Points:\n\n**Admin Listener Creation Flow:**\n- Server initialization calls `admin_->startHttpListener()` (server.cc:721)\n- Creates a dedicated `AdminListener` (AdminImpl::AdminListener class, lines 366-418 in admin.h)\n- Uses `AdminListenSocketFactory` to manage the admin socket\n- Listener added via `admin_->addListenerToHandler()` (admin.cc:524)\n- Listener config uses admin's FilterChainManager/Factory\n\n**Data Plane Listener Creation Flow:**\n- `ListenerManagerImpl::addOrUpdateListener()` processes config from `listeners` section\n- Creates `ListenerImpl` objects\n- Each listener gets network filters defined in config\n- Listener manager adds to workers via `addListenerToWorker()`\n\n### 3. **Network vs HTTP Layer Separation**\n\n**Network Layer (Filter Chain):**\n\nAdmin ↓\nAdminFilter::onComplete() intercepts all requests (admin_filter.cc:83)\n  ↓\nLooks up handler based on URL prefix (admin.cc:381-388)\n  ↓\nCalls matching admin handler or returns 404\n```\n\nData plane HTTP filters:\n- Router filter added last in chain\n- Router filter performs route table lookup and forwards to upstream\n- Request can be modified by other filters first\n\n### 4. **FilterChainManager and Request Routing**\n\n**Admin (Hard-coded routing):**\n\n`AdminImpl` implements both `Network::FilterChainManager` and `Network::FilterChainFactory`:\n- `findFilterChain()` (admin.h:103-105): Returns single `admin_filter_chain_` for all connections\n- `createNetworkFilterChain()`: Creates HttpConnectionManager\n- `createFilterChain()`: Adds only AdminFilter\n- AdminFilter intercepts ALL requests and routes via handler prefix matching\n\n**Data Plane (Route table based):**\n\n`ListenerImpl` uses `FilterChainManagerImpl`:\n- Multiple filter chains can exist per listener\n- Filter chains matched based on SNI, IP, port, etc.\n- Network filters include HttpConnectionManager\n- HttpConnectionManager uses Router filter for request routing\n- Router filter queries `RouteConfigProvider` for dynamic route updates\n\n### 5. **Code Path Analysis: Can Data Plane Reach Admin Handlers?**\n\n**CRITICAL SEPARATION:** No, because:\n\n1. **Listener Isolation:**\n   - Admin listener is a separate `Network::ListenerConfig` instance\n   - Data plane listeners come from `listeners` config section\n   - Each listener has independent socket and filter chain\n   - They listen on different ports/addresses\n\n2. **Filter Chain Segregation:**\n   - Admin's `FilterChainManager` only returns its single `AdminFilterChain`\n   - Admin's `FilterChainFactory` only adds `AdminFilter` to HTTP filter chain\n   - Data plane listeners have their own filter chains from config\n   - Router filter in data plane routes to upstream clusters, not to admin handlers\n\n3. **Handler Registration:**\n   - Admin handlers registered in `AdminImpl::handlers_` list (admin.h:475)\n   - These are only consulted by `AdminFilter::onComplete()` (admin_filter.cc:83)\n   - AdminFilter only exists in admin listener's HTTP filter chain\n   - Data plane requests never enter AdminFilter\n\n4. **Route Configuration:**\n   - Admin uses hard-coded handler routes (prefixes like \"/\", \"/stats\", \"/clusters\")\n   - Data plane uses dynamic route configuration via `RouteConfigProvider`\n   - Route table lookups happen in Router filter, which doesn't know about admin handlers\n   - No code path connects data plane Router to admin handler list\n\n### 6. **Key Files Overview**\n\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| AdminImpl::makeRequest(AdminStream& admin_stream) const {\n   382→  absl::string_view path_and_query = admin_stream.getRequestHeaders().getPathValue();\n   383→  std::string::size_type query_index = path_and_query);\n   402→      return handler.handler_(admin_stream);\n   403→    }\n   404→  }\n   405→\n   406→  // Extra space is emitted below to have \"invalid path.\" be a separate sentence in 466→  // Sanitize prefix and help_text to ensure no XSS can be injected, as\n   467→  // we are injecting these strings into HTML that runs in a domain that\n   468→  // can mutate Envoy server state. 479→    handlers_.push_back({prefix, help_text, callback, removable, mutates_state, params});\n   480→    return true;\n   481→  }\n   482→  return false;\n   483→}\n   484→\n   485→bool
